Preview NoticeThe TypeSpec Assessment Assistant is in preview; official validation and review remain the source of truth.

The TypeSpec Assessment Assistant is currently in preview. Its goal is to help service developers build confidence earlier in the TypeSpec authoring workflow by providing contextual analysis, risk identification, and guidance on potential downstream impacts.

This report is intended as a review reference and learning aid only. It does not replace official ARM API, Azure API Stewardship, Azure Breaking Change reviews, and should not be treated as authoritative review feedback or approval for specification changes. Official validation tools, generated artifacts, and reviewer feedback remain the source of truth for merge and release decisions.

16 of 16 semantic intents assessed · 4 documents selected

New first-party-service-tag suppression omits its justificationThe changed directive omits the justification described by the official #suppress guidance. This finding concerns suppression metadata, not missing documentation on an enum member and not the independently assessed connection-tracking property contract.
Affected intents (1)
Extend shared connection-tracking and public-IP configuration
failed

Expected

Suppress directive allows suppressing a specific warning diagnostic. It takes 2 arguments: the diagnostic code to suppress and a message to justify the suppression.

Directives — #suppress
Suppress directive allows suppressing a specific warning diagnostic. It takes 2 arguments: * The diagnostic code to suppress * A message to justify the suppression

Actual

The new Features.firstPartyServiceTag member has #suppress "@azure-tools/typespec-azure-core/documentation-required" with no second, justification argument.

Downstream breaking changes

Method impact and affected intents at a glance; expand for before/after evidence.

failed3 mapped methods · 0 changed types
FirewallPolicies.createOrUpdateThe normalized SDK method contract changes directly.
Affected intents (1)
Add the AFC-managed firewall-policy synchronization parameter
Direct method change

Target normalized SDK method: Microsoft.Network.FirewallPolicies.createOrUpdate

Contract areaBeforeAfter
SDK methodNormalized input orderFirewallPolicies.createOrUpdate inserts optional afcManagedSync before the required parameters body. In SDKs exposing this order positionally, existing third-argument policy values bind to the new boolean position; callers must adjust arguments or use named arguments.
1. resourceGroupName (path): string
2. firewallPolicyName (path): string
3. parameters (body input): FirewallPolicy
1. resourceGroupName (path): string
2. firewallPolicyName (path): string
3. afcManagedSync? (query): boolean
4. parameters (body input): FirewallPolicy

Constant headers (not numbered caller inputs): Baseline: Content-Type (header): "application/json"; Baseline: Accept (header): "application/json"; Target: Content-Type (header): "application/json"; Target: Accept (header): "application/json"

Why this is breaking: FirewallPolicies.createOrUpdate inserts optional afcManagedSync before the required parameters body. In SDKs exposing this order positionally, existing third-argument policy values bind to the new boolean position; callers must adjust arguments or use named arguments.

Language-neutral TCGC evidence is shown; generated language signatures are not fabricated.

ServiceGateways.updateAddressLocationsThe normalized SDK method contract changes directly.
Affected intents (1)
Replace ServiceGateway update LROs with synchronous actions
Direct method change

Target normalized SDK method: Microsoft.Network.ServiceGateways.updateAddressLocations

Contract areaBeforeAfter
SDK methodNormalized method name
updateAddressLocationsLro
updateAddressLocations
SDK methodLong-running behaviorServiceGateways.updateAddressLocationsLro loses its Location-based polling and void final envelope when replaced by updateAddressLocations. Existing callers that await or inspect LRO completion must switch to consuming the synchronous status response.
{
  "finalStateVia": "location",
  "logicalResult": "Azure.ResourceManager.ArmOperationStatus",
  "pollingStep": "model",
  "finalStep": "finalOperationLink",
  "statusMonitorStep": "nextOperationLink"
}
none
SDK methodMethod kindServiceGateways.updateAddressLocationsLro changes from an LRO to basic updateAddressLocations. Existing SDK callers using polling/completion helpers must migrate to a direct synchronous call; unchanged parameters and routes do not preserve invocation semantics.
lro
basic
SDK methodReturn type (body output)
void
ServiceGatewayActionOkResponseBody

Constant headers (not numbered caller inputs): Baseline: Content-Type (header): "application/json"; Target: Content-Type (header): "application/json"; Target: Accept (header): "application/json"

Why this is breaking:
  • ServiceGateways.updateAddressLocationsLro loses its Location-based polling and void final envelope when replaced by updateAddressLocations. Existing callers that await or inspect LRO completion must switch to consuming the synchronous status response.
  • ServiceGateways.updateAddressLocationsLro becomes updateAddressLocations in the target SDK contract. Existing references to the public LRO method require renaming; source-linked version replacement proves a real SDK identity change despite the preserved REST operation ID.
  • ServiceGateways.updateAddressLocationsLro changes from an LRO to basic updateAddressLocations. Existing SDK callers using polling/completion helpers must migrate to a direct synchronous call; unchanged parameters and routes do not preserve invocation semantics.
  • ServiceGateways.updateAddressLocations changes the prior LRO's void final result into ServiceGatewayActionOkResponseBody. Existing typed LRO-result handling must adopt a synchronous status result rather than the previous completion contract.

Language-neutral TCGC evidence is shown; generated language signatures are not fabricated.

ServiceGateways.updateServicesThe normalized SDK method contract changes directly.
Affected intents (1)
Replace ServiceGateway update LROs with synchronous actions
Direct method change

Target normalized SDK method: Microsoft.Network.ServiceGateways.updateServices

Contract areaBeforeAfter
SDK methodNormalized method name
updateServicesLro
updateServices
SDK methodMethod kindServiceGateways.updateServicesLro changes from an LRO to basic updateServices. Existing SDK code using a poller or LRO completion workflow must change to a direct synchronous invocation; the new version is not equivalent merely because its route is preserved.
lro
basic
SDK methodLong-running behaviorServiceGateways.updateServicesLro loses Location-based polling and its void final envelope when replaced by updateServices. SDK code that polls or awaits an LRO must adopt direct synchronous completion and the returned status body.
{
  "finalStateVia": "location",
  "logicalResult": "Azure.ResourceManager.ArmOperationStatus",
  "pollingStep": "model",
  "finalStep": "finalOperationLink",
  "statusMonitorStep": "nextOperationLink"
}
none
SDK methodReturn type (body output)
void
ServiceGatewayActionOkResponseBody

Constant headers (not numbered caller inputs): Baseline: Content-Type (header): "application/json"; Target: Content-Type (header): "application/json"; Target: Accept (header): "application/json"

Why this is breaking:
  • ServiceGateways.updateServicesLro becomes updateServices in the target SDK contract. Existing source references to the public LRO method require a method-name migration; preserving the REST operation ID does not preserve this TCGC method identity.
  • ServiceGateways.updateServicesLro changes from an LRO to basic updateServices. Existing SDK code using a poller or LRO completion workflow must change to a direct synchronous invocation; the new version is not equivalent merely because its route is preserved.
  • ServiceGateways.updateServicesLro loses Location-based polling and its void final envelope when replaced by updateServices. SDK code that polls or awaits an LRO must adopt direct synchronous completion and the returned status body.
  • ServiceGateways.updateServices changes the prior LRO's void final result into ServiceGatewayActionOkResponseBody. Existing typed LRO-result handling must migrate to the synchronous status result; this is not an implicit-to-explicit wrapper preserving the same SDK member.

Language-neutral TCGC evidence is shown; generated language signatures are not fabricated.

Add first-party service tags and related SDK naming metadataAdds the FirstPartyServiceTag resource and ARM read, create-or-replace, tag-update, delete, resource-group list, and subscription-list templates for 2025-09-01. This supplied coarse group also retains explicit operation IDs for the older ServiceGateway LRO variants and an SDK endpoint-type name customization; these are not one proven business feature.

+@added(Versions.v2025_09_01)
+union LabelSelectorOperator {
+  /** The label value must be in the supplied set of values. */
+  In: "In",
+
+  /** The label value must not be in the supplied set of values. */
+  NotIn: "NotIn",
+
+  /** The label key must exist, regardless of its value. */
+  Exists: "Exists",
+
+  /** The label key must not exist. */
+  DoesNotExist: "DoesNotExist",
+
+  string,
+}

+/**
+ * The type of diagnostic operation to run on a connection analyzer.
+ */
+@added(Versions.v2025_09_01)
+union DiagnosticOperation {
+  string,
+
+  /**
+   * Determines the next hop for traffic from the source to the destination.
+   */
+  NextHop: "NextHop",
+
+  /**
+   * Evaluates the network security group rules that apply to the traffic.
+   */
+  NSG: "NSG",
+
+  /**
+   * Scans the destination ports to determine their reachability.
+   */
+  PortScan: "PortScan",
+
+  /**
+   * Performs an end-to-end connectivity check between the source and destination.
+   */
+  ConnectivityCheck: "ConnectivityCheck",
+
+  /**
+   * Runs Express Route specific diagnostics for the connection.
+   */
+  ExpressRouteDiagnostic: "ExpressRouteDiagnostic",
+}
+
+namespace ConnectionAnalyzerEndpointTypes {
+  /**
+   * The endpoint type for a connection analyzer source or destination.
+   */
+  @added(Versions.v2025_09_01)
+  union EndpointType {
+    string,
+
+    /**
+     * An Azure virtual machine endpoint, identified by its ARM resource ID.
+     */
+    VM: "VM",
+
+    /**
+     * An Azure virtual machine scale set (or scale set instance) endpoint, identified by its ARM resource ID.
+     */
+    VMSS: "VMSS",
+
+    /**
+     * An external endpoint reachable from outside Azure, identified by an IP address or FQDN.
+     */
+    ExternalAddress: "ExternalAddress",
+
+    /**
+     * An Azure Bastion host endpoint, identified by its ARM resource ID.
+     */
+    BastionHost: "BastionHost",
+
+    /**
+     * An Application Gateway endpoint, identified by its ARM resource ID.
+     */
+    ApplicationGateway: "ApplicationGateway",
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Current execution status of the connection analyzer.
+ */
+@added(Versions.v2025_09_01)
+union ConnectionAnalyzerStatus {
+  string,
+
+  /**
+   * The connection analyzer has been created but execution has not yet begun.
+   */
+  NotStarted: "NotStarted",
+
+  /**
+   * The connection analyzer is currently running its diagnostic operations.
+   */
+  Running: "Running",
+
+  /**
+   * The connection analyzer completed all diagnostic operations successfully.
+   */
+  Succeeded: "Succeeded",
+
+  /**
+   * The connection analyzer failed to complete its diagnostic operations.
+   */
+  Failed: "Failed",
+}

+#suppress "@azure-tools/typespec-azure-core/no-legacy-usage" "Required to attach Connection Analyzer child models to the existing NetworkWatcher resource which is modeled with the legacy ARM resource pattern via Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.feature."
+@added(Versions.v2025_09_01)
+@Azure.ResourceManager.Legacy.feature(Features.networkWatcher)
+@doc("""
+  Describes a source or destination endpoint of a connection analyzer.
+  
+  The schema is intentionally permissive (a flat object) because the set of
+  valid fields is determined by `type` and is enforced server-side by the
+  Network Resource Provider. The required and allowed fields per `type` are:
+  
+  - `VM`, `VMSS`, `BastionHost`, `ApplicationGateway`: `resourceId` is
+    required and must be the ARM resource ID of the corresponding resource
+    (a virtual machine, virtual machine scale set or VMSS instance, Azure
+    Bastion host, or Application Gateway, respectively); `address` is not
+    used; `port` is optional.
+  - `ExternalAddress`: `address` is required and must be an IP address or
+    FQDN reachable from outside Azure; `resourceId` is not used; `port` is
+    optional.
+  
+  Requests that supply fields not allowed for the chosen `type`, or omit a
+  required field, are rejected by the service with a validation error. This
+  follows the same convention used by `ConnectivitySource` and
+  `ConnectivityDestination` on Network Watcher.
+  """)
+model ConnectionAnalyzerEndpoint {
+  /**
+   * The type of the endpoint. Determines which of `resourceId` and `address`
+   * are required; see the model-level documentation for the per-type contract.
+   */
+  type: ConnectionAnalyzerEndpointTypes.EndpointType;
+
+  /**
+   * ARM resource ID of the endpoint resource. Required when `type` is `VM`,
+   * `VMSS`, `BastionHost`, or `ApplicationGateway`. Not used when `type` is
+   * `ExternalAddress`.
+   */
+  resourceId?: armResourceIdentifier;
+
+  /**
+   * IP address or FQDN of the endpoint. Required when `type` is
+   * `ExternalAddress`. Not used for the resource-typed endpoint kinds (`VM`,
+   * `VMSS`, `BastionHost`, `ApplicationGateway`).
+   */
+  address?: string;
+
+  /**
+   * Port to use for the endpoint (0-65535).
+   */
+  @minValue(0)
+  @maxValue(65535)
+  port?: int32;
+}

Add the network-virtual-appliance migration workflowAdds prepare, execute, commit, and abort migration actions on NetworkVirtualAppliance for 2025-09-01 using ArmResourceActionAsyncBase. The templates explicitly permit accepted asynchronous responses or successful no-content completion; prepare/execute/commit use migration request models, while abort has no request payload.
